Plan version: `goal-v3`, owner-approved M2-v2 candidate-coverage amendment 2026-10-10; see sections DJ/DK. Earlier goal-v2 M1 completion remains valid, and original goal-v2 M2 A/B are superseded without PASS.
Ultimate goal status: **NOT COMPLETE**. M0 and M1-v2 **COMPLETE**; owner-approved M2-v2 successor Prompt A promoted/eligible but not executed; M3–M7 PENDING.

## A. Immutable Project Charter — WHY

Build a trustworthy, reusable **TWSE-only market-opening segment** for the daily TAIWAN STOCK 5%-gainers YouTube video and, after evidence-backed verification, integrate it into the existing daily automation without duplicate work. A final success is not merely a green CI run or a JSON sample: the segment must appear correctly in a real end-to-end daily video, with factually valid market interpretation, aligned narration/TTS/Traditional Chinese subtitles, visual preview, and durable daily replay/upload behavior.

The first three spoken lines, exactly in order, must be:
1. 大家好，歡迎來到 TAIWAN STOCK！
2. 追蹤資金，解讀行情。
3. 錢在哪，我們就在哪！

The **entire market-opening segment**, including those brand lines, must be **60–90 seconds** measured with actual TTS. Style: 70% professional financial anchor, 30% incisive analyst. Spoken content, display cues, and Traditional Chinese SRT must have one semantic source; SRT must not omit spoken sentences. Do not use unverified facts, invented figures, or assert retail/institutional counterparty trades without evidence.

## B. Scope and frozen acceptance conditions — WHAT

- Market and breadth: TWSE listed **ordinary common stock only**. Exclude TPEx, ETFs, ETNs, warrants, DRs, preferred shares, and other non-common instruments. Never infer identity from code length.
- Report official TAIEX O/H/L/C, point/% change; *separately scoped* official TWSE trading value/volume/count and 5-trading-day average; common-stock advancers/decliners/unchanged/no-trade and >=5% gainers; three institutional groups' **TWD** buy/sell/net; defensible industry turnover and market mood with evidence and explicit uncertainty.
- No conflation of TWSE whole-market turnover with ordinary-stock-only turnover. No conflation of individual-stock **shares** with institution **TWD** net flows, or sector turnover with net capital inflow.
- Daily source dates and versioned source manifests must be checked; no silent prior-day fallback on missing data or non-trading days.
- The project must not alter V1/V2 predictions, existing market-summary, protected workflow routing, existing YouTube upload/resume behavior, or all other tasks except through separately accepted scoped changes.
- Fail closed if classifications or critical dates are unproven. `partial` is NOT final publication authorization. Do not silently drop missing fields from the required feature contract to claim success.
- Reuse current daily video source and script/TTS/caption architecture. Production automation integration only after real preview/video evidence and explicit scoped verification.
- Every round must document actual outcome and current remote main, not infer PASS from commits or conversation.

## F. Scope-change and plan-version governance (proposal implemented within THIS task handoff)

All AI agents shall uphold the Charter and roadmap. If an unanticipated requirement, missing authoritative source, new security risk, or technical constraint arises, agent must:
1. Log `CHANGE_PROPOSAL`: observed evidence, why original plan fails, affected phases, options, tradeoffs, requested acceptance change, no speculative commitments.
2. Classify as `implementation detail` (within frozen goal and acceptance) or `scope/goal/acceptance change`.
3. Minor implementation detail may be handled within the active paired contract with durable record and Prompt B recheck; it must not silently alter outcomes.
4. Scope/goal/acceptance change requires owner discussion and explicit approval **before** rewriting the Charter or phase acceptance. Append `decision log`, bump plan version, preserve prior contract/history. Do not retroactively relax an active round's preregistered Prompt B; close as BLOCKED/SUPERSEDED with owner approval and a new pair.
5. A new phase must link back to a specific Charter requirement and prove it is necessary. No open-ended research tangents or unapproved production rollout.
6. Every closeout must report `ultimate goal progress`, `finished stages`, `remaining blockers`, `next phase id` and `whether roadmap changed`.

## M. M1 free historical TWSE official breadth baseline discovered — 2026-10-09

**NEW EVIDENCE (not M1 PASS):** TWSE's public, historical, date-addressable MI_INDEX official report contains a distinct **股票** column under `漲跌證券數合計`, in addition to the all-securities figures. On the TWSE official page for **115年10月08日**, the 股票 column reports 上漲 425 (漲停 14), 下跌 540 (跌停 2), 持平 109, 未成交 3, 無比價 5. Official source: `https://www.twse.com.tw/exchangeReport/MI_INDEX?date=20261008&response=html`; retrieved page `https://www.twse.com.tw/exchangeReport/MI_INDEX?amp=&response=html` explicitly displayed 115年10月08日. Treat as **official aggregate benchmark** only, pending reproducible date-specific request capture and full scope semantics. The five category counts total 1,082; **無比價 is a distinct TWSE class, NOT equivalent to 未成交**. Source also separately breaks out trading turnover for 一般股票, 變更交易股票, 創新板股票, ETF, TDR, ETN and warrants, so aggregates must not be blended across scopes.

Additional legitimate free historical references: TWSE `https://www.twse.com.tw/rwd/en/company/suspendListing?response=html` and `https://www.twse.com.tw/company/suspendListingCsvAndHtml?lang=zh&type=html` provide dated delistings, helpful for detecting survivorship bias but not sufficient for a complete as-of security-type master. TWSE `https://www.twse.com.tw/zh/trading/historical/mi-index.html` offers historical daily reports / CSV download. Government open-data datasets `https://data.gov.tw/dataset/18419` (monthly current listed-company master), `https://data.gov.tw/dataset/11425` (daily current TDCC security master), and `https://data.gov.tw/dataset/28567` (daily public-issuer profile) do not by mere update frequency prove preservation of the exact 20261008 original archives. TWSE ISIN classification search `https://isin.twse.com.tw/isin/class_i.jsp?kind=1` offers security-type filtering but no verified point-in-time historical query.

**Required next bounded implementation investigation:** capture and hash an authenticated exact 20261008 free TWSE official report response (record canonical date-filtered URL, response date, archival original bytes, extraction/format, and source scope); inspect the 10-table archived MI_INDEX already in repo for this aggregate section and per-quote categories. Independently determine whether official 股票 counts include innovation-board, altered-trading-method stocks and other exceptional common-stock classifications, and whether `無比價` requires an added verified bucket to the original breadth contract; report any contract/acceptance amendment as owner-gated CHANGE_PROPOSAL, NEVER silently relax original M1 Prompt B. Reconcile aggregate 425/540/109/3/5 with independently classified individual rows; use official delisting records plus archived issuer/instrument evidence if a genuine full historical master becomes available. If complete dated issuer-level classification still cannot be established, remain BLOCKED_ON_HISTORICAL_MASTER_PROVENANCE, preserve `partial`, and do not fabricate the 5%-gainers count or move to M2.

**research_progress_status: PROGRESS; phase M1 BLOCKED_ON_HISTORICAL_MASTER_PROVENANCE; A NOT COMPLETE/B NOT ELIGIBLE.** Verified remote main `6286ad011d9afad8e9928ecd5f97efd53b295c2e`, unique active Goal v1 task, and current blocker. The ISIN system's mode `strMode=11` domestic index registry shows updated 2026/10/08 but is likewise unrelated to listed issuer completeness. ## AO. M1 official FIX/FAST security-status codes clarify trading halt versus suspension — 2026-10-10

**research_progress_status: PROGRESS; M1 phase: BLOCKED_ON_HISTORICAL_MASTER_PROVENANCE; Prompt A INCOMPLETE; Prompt B NOT ELIGIBLE.** Checked remote main `492afc883bc1f7a5df4bcf866713dee4e09a0fc4` and globally unique Active task. Directly read original TWSE `FIX/FAST 資訊傳輸系統 連線作業手冊 V1.12` (2012 edition), `https://www.twse.com.tw/docs1/data01/market/public_html/1010917-1010602204-1.pdf`, PDF page 42 (zero-index page 41) and security-status `f1` schema. The exchange distinguishes security description **T** = ceased trading due to delisting, **S** = ceased trading due to suspension, **H** = intraday trading halt, **R** = intraday resume, plus special anomaly/monitoring flags. Crucially the explanatory text states same-day stopped-trading stocks are **not contained in that day's basic security information**. This is direct, authoritative historical protocol support for the existing rule that 1589/2323/4155 can be in a dated listing/category roster but **absent from the quote/day basic-info files** without being `no_trade`, `OTHER` or already delisted. It also demonstrates why a `TWTAWU` historical **trading halt event** table must not be casually assumed to enumerate all multi-day `S` suspensions: it is a different data family and its dated query result remains unverified. Verified public TWSE `https://www.twse.com.tw/zh/trading/historical/twtawu.html` supports historical-date search and CSV export but 2026-10-08 CSV was **not successfully retrieved**; the attempted TWSE `/rwd/zh/exchangeReport/TWTAWU?date=20261008&response=json` URL was not accessible through the web reader. This PDF is a 2012 schema reference, **not** proof that the exact 2026 production status stream or security master was archived/obtained.

**Difference from last round:** hard first-party formal field semantics `S/T/H/R` and explicit daily-basic-security exclusion for halted stocks; prior discovery only identified endpoint names. **Current blockers:** actual as-of-20261008 original full issuer/type/listing-date security master; exact-dated status stream/CSV provenance if relying on it; complete TIB/changed-method crosschecks. **Planned research:** prioritize owner-provided original 20261008 TWSE dated security master or licensed status feed (including T/S with timestamps) and use status schema only to validate actual source files; no speculative endpoint guess and no more duplicate CI. **Stop/pivot:** if neither officially archived listed security universe nor authoritative historical event data are obtainable, record BLOCKED with explicit owner source acquisition decision rather than pretending free historical page is exhaustive. ## BI. Source-only historical master provenance and authoritative 3673 CFI semantics — 2026-10-10

**Research progress: PROGRESS, M1 original BLOCKED_ON_HISTORICAL_MASTER_PROVENANCE, Prompt B NOT ELIGIBLE.** Preflight remote main `ba6fc87472ad13cf2656902382e35c2a7bdd6508` and sole Active `daily-gainers-market-opening-end-to-end` checked. Only public first-party research and Handoff are within scope; **no CI/production/cron/frozen-acceptance modifications**.

**3673 CFI classification meaning now independently corroborated**: TWSE ISIN `https://isin.twse.com.tw/isin/class_main.jsp?issuetype=1` explicitly assigns `ESVTFR` to `3673 TPK-KY` as a listed **equity stock**, ISIN `KYG898431096`, date listed `2010/10/29`. ISO official technical note `https://committee.iso.org/sites/tc68/home/news/content-left-area/news-and-updates/add-a-post-5.html` explains six-character E-S-V-U-F-R code where E = equities, S = common/ordinary shares, V = voting, U = unrestricted, F = fully paid, R = registered. Thailand Stock Exchange official CFI explainer `https://media.set.or.th/set/Documents/2022/Mar/CFIContent_20170911.pdf` provides **exact ESVTFR example** for common voting, **ownership/transfer restrictions (T)**, fully paid, registered, versus U unrestricted. Euronext `https://live.euronext.com/en/product/equities/NO0010331838-XOSL/market-information` independently labels exact ESVTFR as ordinary shares with restricted ownership/transfer. ISO 10962:2021 official standards overview `https://www.iso.org/standard/81140.html` acknowledges instrument characteristic changes can cause CFI reclassification. **Bounded conclusion:** ESVTFR is a category of ordinary/common share with restrictions; it is NOT categorically excluded from a common-share universe merely for having T instead of U. But official ISO CFI semantics, as observed on later ISIN source page, still do **NOT independently certify its exact 20261008 historic CFI/effective listing status**; original M1 acceptance remains blocked. Do not equate ownership restriction with exchange imposed trading suspension.

**Historical identity and transition sources investigated:** TWSE official `https://www.twse.com.tw/zh/focus/lt185-dtr.html` is a historical listed common-share count/TDR unit change announcement service, filtering announcement dates, **effective dates** and code, published records since Sep 2003. It is valuable for share-capital transitions, but cannot establish exhaustive all listed stock identity/type and listing/board movements on Oct 8–10. TWSE `https://dsp.twse.com.tw/official/search` offers searchable official circulars from Oct 2020 including listing notices, but no verified exhaustive 20261008–20261010 event export/coverage contract found. `https://www.twse.com.tw/zh/announcement/announcement/list.html` is a first-party public announcement index; its observed listings confirm future-effect delisting of 1589 announced Oct 8, not a security-status change effective that day. Previous owner files: dated Oct8 company-level `t187ap03_L.json` report with 1085 ISIN stock identity matches and 10 TDR extra vs Oct10 ISIN Big5 snapshot. These independently narrow doubt but remain different effective-date/master scopes. No reliable public endpoint permitting arbitrary `20261008` historical as-of **all-security CFI/board** snapshot was authenticated this round; importantly, **absence found in research is not proof one does not exist**.

**Difference vs prior:** 3673 precise ESVTFR taxonomy resolved at international classification level, and two further first-party date-filtered research surfaces identified with coverage caveats. **Current blocker:** source-authenticated original complete 20261008 listed ordinary security master OR exhaustive effective-dated event-ledger certification of no 20261008→20261010 status changes; for 3673 original as-of target date attribution (not code meaning) still absent. **Planned research direction:** inspect official circular query's pagination and coverage for 20261008–20261010, correlate all effective-date change categories (listing/delisting/board transfer/corporate actions), and seek a paid or legitimately licensed historical daily identity master if complete exchange archival evidence remains unavailable. **Stop/pivot:** never declare the Oct10 list automatically effective on Oct8; never remove 3673 on CFI T alone; do not promote M1 or Prompt B, and do not modify production/cron/Pages/YouTube. M0 COMPLETE; M1 BLOCKED; M2–M7 PENDING. NEXT_COMMAND: `promptA` to test bounded official effective-date event-index completeness and as-of Oct8 source-provenance contract, without changing acceptance.

### M2-v2 A — preregistered research implementation contract (freeze here)

Disclose counts and uncertainty explicitly; do not assert entire TWSE common-stock universe complete. Tests must cover real archived source rows, reject fabricated identity, instrument cross-contamination (TDR, ETF, ETN, warrants, preferred), stale source, miscomputed >=5%, zero/invalid denominator, duplicate codes, unknown/innovation category ambiguity, major uncovered candidate and improper final publication. 6. Prompt A COMPLETE requires durable code, research audit, test evidence and Handoff checkpoint, with no unresolved **material** candidate or featured-stock gate, and reproducible scoped financial facts. A COMPLETE never authorizes video publication. If any required output is not proven, mark BLOCKED or IN_PROGRESS and continue the *same* A; no invented completeness.

### M2-v2 B — independent preregistered acceptance contract (immutable before A)

1. On a fresh main, independently recover this **exact** M2-v2 A/B version and tested commit, confirm unique Active and scope exclusions. No silent contract change.
2. Independently replay original byte-pinned sources, recompute candidate extraction, crosscheck Stock/InnovationBoard/TDR/ETF categorization and explicit unknown cases. 5. Confirm zero production/video/YouTube/Pages/cron/deployment changes, provenance and no unapproved user-facing claims. Otherwise B FAIL/BLOCKED with bounded repair under same criteria. No retroactive B relaxation.

**CHANGE_PROPOSAL approved by owner in conversation on 2026-10-10.** Owner directive: keep strict real dated stock price, ordinary-stock identity, original >=5% threshold and provenance; change disposition and temporary trading halt from universal exclusion into **explicit risk disclosure**, and do not automatically suppress an important featured stock when risk source is missing. The historical M2-v2 paired A/B contract in DK remains IMMUTABLE; its old risk-based eligibility rule and historical incomplete status are preserved and are **SUPERSEDED, NOT PASSED**. Start a new forward-only amendment contract identifier **M2-v2-RISK-DISCLOSURE-v1** for the still-current M2 research phase, under goal-v3. No historic B or future M3 criteria are retroactively marked PASS. M0/M1-v2 prior accepted; M2-v2 remains active and in progress; M3–M7 remain pending.

### New paired Prompt A — M2-v2-RISK-DISCLOSURE-v1, preregistered before amended implementation

1. Re-fetch latest main, unique Active, read this owner decision. Research-only changes to scoped validation, fixtures, tests and research handoff; NEVER change production/video/upload/Pages/cron/deployment or run Prompt B automatically.
2. Maintain hard gates for actual 20261008 official same-date individual quote, correctly classified ordinary share (not ETF, ETN, preferred, warrant or TDR), original actual unchanged >=5% arithmetic, zero/invalid denominator, source integrity and source dates. Unknown core identity/price remains **not eligible**. Do not substitute a true Boolean or hypothetical fixture for authentic source proof. Preserve separate market breadth and source-pinned finance facts.
3. Disposition and temporary halt each return **KNOWN_ACTIVE / VERIFIED_NONE / UNKNOWN** with source and as-of date. Known active restrictions are NOT automatically excluded from a narrative about observed strength, but mandate accurate context and risk wording. UNKNOWN remains eligible only when **all hard core conditions are actually proven**, and must be explicitly disclosed without a fabricated negative assertion. Actual untradable/suspended quote anomalies, missing or stale price, and unverified core identities still fail the hard gate; describe this distinction in research output.
4. Preserve the original strict legacy per-security checker unchanged for audit/backward comparability. Implement a new versioned core-market-data validator removing only the disposition/suspension clearance requirement from the **narrative consideration gate**; do not silently alter original strict gate behavior or falsely label its PASS. No change to original threshold or computation.
5. Research-only candidate output must distinguish `core_eligible`, `risk_disclosure_required`, `narrative_consideration_eligible`, and `publication_authorized=false`. Show explanatory reason flags and risk status; no automatic publication.
6. Regression cover official source-backed risk cases, active disposition, unknown disposition/halt, false core identity, non-ordinary instruments, stale dates, calculated <5%, wrong proof percentage, duplicate code, and a case where halted/non-trading evidence undermines the price claim.
